<!-- SECURITY AUDIT FOOTER & ENCRYPTION BADGE -->
<div class="px-gutter pb-space-lg flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center gap-space-xxs">
<div class="flex items-center gap-space-xxs text-tertiary">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">lock</span>
<span class="font-label-code text-label-code uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">PAD Insight Security Framework v2.4</span>
</div>
<p class="font-label-status text-label-status text-on-surface-variant max-w-sm">
      Enkripsi Berbasis Role-Based Access Control (RBAC) • Standar Keamanan Data Bapenda Pemkab Ogan Komering Ulu Selatan
    </p>
</div>

<script>
  // Simple client-side micro-interactions for Role Filters and Search
  const roleTabs = document.querySelectorAll('.role-tab');
  const userCards = document.querySelectorAll('[data-user-card]');
  const searchInput = document.getElementById('user-search-input');

  roleTabs.forEach(tab => {
    tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
      roleTabs.forEach(t => {
        t.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary', 'shadow-sm');
        t.classList.add('text-on-surface-variant');
      });
      tab.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary', 'shadow-sm');
      tab.classList.remove('text-on-surface-variant');

      const selectedRole = tab.getAttribute('data-role');
      userCards.forEach(card => {
        if (selectedRole === 'all' || card.getAttribute('data-user-card') === selectedRole) {
          card.style.display = 'flex';
        } else {
          card.style.display = 'none';
        }
      });
    });
  });

  if (searchInput) {
    searchInput.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
      userCards.forEach(card => {
        const text = card.textContent.toLowerCase();
        if (text.includes(query)) {
          card.style.display = 'flex';
        } else {
          card.style.display = 'none';
        }
      });
    });
  }

  // Toggle quick user creation form accordion
  const formHeaderToggle = document.getElementById('form-header-toggle');
  const btnToggleCreateUser = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-create-user');
  const quickCreateForm = document.getElementById('quick-create-form');
  const formToggleIcon = document.getElementById('form-toggle-icon');

  function toggleForm() {
    if (quickCreateForm.classList.contains('hidden')) {
      quickCreateForm.classList.remove('hidden');
      formToggleIcon.textContent = 'expand_less';
      quickCreateForm.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    } else {
      quickCreateForm.classList.add('hidden');
      formToggleIcon.textContent = 'expand_more';
    }
  }

  if (formHeaderToggle) {
    formHeaderToggle.addEventListener('click', toggleForm);
  }
  if (btnToggleCreateUser) {
    btnToggleCreateUser.addEventListener('click', () => {
      quickCreateForm.classList.remove('hidden');
      formToggleIcon.textContent = 'expand_less';
      quickCreateForm.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    });
  }
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-50 pb-safe bg-surface/80 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_1px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-semibold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-16 px-space-xs"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="dashboard" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">pie_chart</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Dashboard</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="data-pendapatan" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">table_chart</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Data</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="analisis-pad" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">query_stats</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Analisis</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="laporan" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">description</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Laporan</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="pengaturan" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">settings</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Pengaturan</span></a></div></nav><script>const openBtn=document.getElementById('btn-open-drawer');const closeBtn=document.getElementById('btn-close-drawer');const drawer=document.getElementById('nav-drawer');const backdrop=document.getElementById('nav-drawer-backdrop');function toggleDrawer(open){if(open){drawer.classList.remove('-translate-x-full');backdrop.classList.remove('hidden');}else{drawer.classList.add('-translate-x-full');backdrop.classList.add('hidden');}}if(openBtn){openBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>toggleDrawer(true));}if(closeBtn){closeBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>toggleDrawer(false));}if(backdrop){backdrop.addEventListener('click',()=>toggleDrawer(false));}</script></body></html>

<script>
  // Filter chips tab interaktif
  const chips = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip');
  chips.forEach(chip => {
    chip.addEventListener('click', () => {
      chips.forEach(c => {
        c.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary', 'shadow-sm');
        c.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low', 'text-on-surface-variant');
      });
      chip.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary', 'shadow-sm');
      chip.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-low', 'text-on-surface-variant');
    });
  });

  // Toggle Minimize Form
  const btnMinForm = document.getElementById('btn-minimize-form');
  const formBody = document.getElementById('form-body');
  let formOpen = true;

  btnMinForm.addEventListener('click', () => {
    formOpen = !formOpen;
    if (formOpen) {
      formBody.classList.remove('hidden');
      btnMinForm.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">expand_less</span>';
    } else {
      formBody.classList.add('hidden');
      btnMinForm.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">expand_more</span>';
    }
  });

  // Scroll to Form on Tambah Data Click
  const btnOpenForm = document.getElementById('btn-open-form');
  btnOpenForm.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (!formOpen) {
      formOpen = true;
      formBody.classList.remove('hidden');
      btnMinForm.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">expand_less</span>';
    }
    document.getElementById('quick-entry-container').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  });

  // Toast Notifikasi Trigger
  const toast = document.getElementById('toast-notif');
  const toastText = document.getElementById('toast-text');
  const toastClose = document.getElementById('toast-close');
  const btnSaveData = document.getElementById('btn-save-data');

  function showToast(msg) {
    toastText.textContent = msg;
    toast.classList.remove('translate-y-32');
    setTimeout(() => {
      toast.classList.add('translate-y-32');
    }, 3500);
  }

  btnSaveData.addEventListener('click', () => {
    showToast('Data PAD OKUS 2024 berhasil tersimpan di sistem!');
  });

  toastClose.addEventListener('click', () => {
    toast.classList.add('translate-y-32');
  });
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-50 pb-safe bg-surface/80 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_1px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-semibold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-16 px-space-xs"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="dashboard" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">pie_chart</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Dashboard</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col text-[22px]">table_chart</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Data</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12   // Dynamic toast interaction for comparative filter
  const compareBtn = document.getElementById('btn-compare-trigger');
  if (compareBtn) {
    compareBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const originalText = compareBtn.innerHTML;
      compareBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] animate-spin">refresh</span><span>Mengolah Komparasi Telemetri...</span>';
      compareBtn.classList.add('opacity-90');
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        compareBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">check_circle</span><span>Matriks Terkoreksi (Simulasi OKUS)</span>';
        compareBtn.classList.remove('opacity-90');
        setTimeout(() => {
          compareBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
        }, 2200);
      }, 1000);
    });
  }

  // Export action microfeedback
  const exportBtn = document.getElementById('btn-export-pdf');
  if (exportBtn) {
    exportBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const prev = exportBtn.innerHTML;
      exportBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] animate-spin">sync</span>';
      setTimeout(() => {
        exportBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">done</span>';
        setTimeout(() => {
          exportBtn.innerHTML = prev;
        }, 1500);
      }, 800);
    });

<!-- Interactive Client-side Script for Feedback -->
<script>
    function triggerExport(format, colorClasses) {
      const toast = document.getElementById('export-toast');
      const text = document.getElementById('toast-text');
      if (!toast || !text) return;
      
      text.innerText = `Menghasilkan file ${format}... Siap diunduh`;
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.add('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2');
        toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
      }, 2600);
    }

    function selectPeriod(btn) {
      const container = document.getElementById('filter-period-tabs');
      if (!container) return;
      const buttons = container.querySelectorAll('button');
      buttons.forEach(b => {
        b.className = 'h-8 rounded-lg text-on-surface-variant font-label-status text-label-status transition-all text-center';
      });
      btn.className = 'h-8 rounded-lg bg-surface-container-lowest text-primary font-label-status text-label-status shadow-sm transition-all text-center font-semibold';
      
      const toast = document.getElementById('export-toast');
      const text = document.getElementById('toast-text');
      if (toast && text) {
        text.innerText = `Periode diperbarui: ${btn.innerText}`;
        toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2');
        toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
        setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.add('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2');
          toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
        }, 1800);
      }
    }
  </script>

justify-around items-center h-16 px-space-xs"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="dashboard" <!-- BEGIN: Interactive Logic Scripts -->
<script data-purpose="form-interactions">
    // Toggle Password Visibility
    const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggle-password');
    const passwordInput = document.getElementById('user-password');
    let isVisible = false;

    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      isVisible = !isVisible;
      passwordInput.type = isVisible ? 'text' : 'password';
      toggleBtn.classList.toggle('text-blue-600', isVisible);
      toggleBtn.classList.toggle('text-slate-400', !isVisible);
    });

    // Demo Role Selector Helper
    function selectRole(email, roleLabel, element) {
      // Set input value
      const nipInput = document.getElementById('user-nip');
      nipInput.value = email;

      // Reset styling for all role buttons
      const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('.role-btn');
      buttons.forEach(btn => {
        btn.classList.remove('border-blue-300', 'bg-white', 'shadow-xs');
        btn.classList.add('border-slate-200', 'bg-white/70');
      });

      // Highlight active button
      element.classList.remove('border-slate-200', 'bg-white/70');
      element.classList.add('border-blue-300', 'bg-white', 'shadow-xs');

      // Visual focus feedback on input
      nipInput.focus();
      setTimeout(() => {
        nipInput.blur();
      }, 400);
    }
  </script>
<!-- END: Interactive Logic Scripts -->

<script>
  // Drawer interactions
  const openBtn = document.getElementById('btn-open-drawer');
  const closeBtn = document.getElementById('btn-close-drawer');
  const drawer = document.getElementById('nav-drawer');
  const backdrop = document.getElementById('nav-drawer-backdrop');

  function toggleDrawer(open) {
    if (open) {
      drawer.classList.remove('-translate-x-full');
      backdrop.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else {
      drawer.classList.add('-translate-x-full');
      backdrop.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }

  if (openBtn) openBtn.addEventListener('click', () => toggleDrawer(true));
  if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => toggleDrawer(false));
  if (backdrop) backdrop.addEventListener('click', () => toggleDrawer(false));

  // Slider visual simulation and sync
  const sliderTercapai = document.getElementById('slider-tercapai');
  const sliderMendekati = document.getElementById('slider-mendekati');
  const labelTercapai = document.getElementById('label-tercapai');
  const labelMendekati = document.getElementById('label-mendekati');
  const trackKritis = document.getElementById('track-kritis');
  const trackMendekati = document.getElementById('track-mendekati');
  const trackTercapai = document.getElementById('track-tercapai');
  const tagKritis = document.getElementById('tag-kritis');
  const tagMendekati = document.getElementById('tag-mendekati');
  const tagTercapai = document.getElementById('tag-tercapai');
  const kritisDescLabel = document.getElementById('kritis-desc-label');

  function updateThresholdPreview() {
    let tVal = parseFloat(sliderTercapai.value);
    let mVal = parseFloat(sliderMendekati.value);
    if (mVal >= tVal) {
      mVal = tVal - 1;
      sliderMendekati.value = mVal;
    }
    labelTercapai.innerText = tVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    labelMendekati.innerText = mVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    if (kritisDescLabel) kritisDescLabel.innerText = 'Ambang Kritis: < ' + mVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    
    // update preview track
    if (trackKritis) trackKritis.style.width = mVal + '%';
    if (trackMendekati) trackMendekati.style.width = Math.max(0, tVal - mVal) + '%';
    if (trackTercapai) trackTercapai.style.width = Math.max(0, 100 - tVal) + '%';

    if (tagKritis) tagKritis.innerText = '<' + Math.round(mVal) + '%';
    if (tagMendekati) tagMendekati.innerText = Math.round(mVal) + '-' + Math.round(tVal - 1) + '%';
    if (tagTercapai) tagTercapai.innerText = '≥' + Math.round(tVal) + '%';

    // modal reflection
    const modalValT = document.getElementById('modal-val-tercapai');
    const modalValM = document.getElementById('modal-val-mendekati');
    if (modalValT) modalValT.innerText = tVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    if (modalValM) modalValM.innerText = mVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
  }

  if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.addEventListener('input', updateThresholdPreview);
  if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.addEventListener('input', updateThresholdPreview);

  // Modal & Toast elements
  const modal = document.getElementById('modal-konfirmasi');
  const modalContent = document.getElementById('modal-konfirmasi-content');
  const btnTrigger = document.getElementById('btn-trigger-save-modal');
  const btnCloseX = document.getElementById('btn-close-modal-x');
  const btnCancel = document.getElementById('btn-cancel-modal');
  const btnConfirm = document.getElementById('btn-confirm-submit');
  const toast = document.getElementById('toast-success-config');
  const btnCloseToast = document.getElementById('btn-close-toast');
  const toastDesc = document.getElementById('toast-desc');

  let toastTimer = null;

  function showToast(message) {
    if (toastDesc && message) {
      toastDesc.innerText = message;
    }
    if (toast) {
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2', 'pointer-events-none');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
      if (toastTimer) clearTimeout(toastTimer);
      toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        hideToast();
      }, 5000);
    }
  }

  function hideToast() {
    if (toast) {
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2', 'pointer-events-none');
    }
  }

  function openConfirmationModal() {
    updateThresholdPreview();
    if (modal) {
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      // microtask/RAF for smooth CSS transition
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        modal.classList.add('opacity-100');
        if (modalContent) {
          modalContent.classList.remove('scale-95');
          modalContent.classList.add('scale-100');
        }
      });
    }
  }

  function closeConfirmationModal() {
    if (modal) {
      modal.classList.remove('opacity-100');
      modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      if (modalContent) {
        modalContent.classList.remove('scale-100');
        modalContent.classList.add('scale-95');
      }
      setTimeout(() => {
        modal.classList.add('hidden');
      }, 300);
    }
  }

  if (btnTrigger) {
    btnTrigger.addEventListener('click', openConfirmationModal);
  }
  if (btnCloseX) {
    btnCloseX.addEventListener('click', closeConfirmationModal);
  }
  if (btnCancel) {
    btnCancel.addEventListener('click', closeConfirmationModal);
  }
  if (modal) {
    modal.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === modal) {
        closeConfirmationModal();
      }
    });
  }

  if (btnConfirm) {
    btnConfirm.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const valT = sliderTercapai ? parseFloat(sliderTercapai.value).toFixed(1) + '%' : '80.0%';
      const valM = sliderMendekati ? parseFloat(sliderMendekati.value).toFixed(1) + '%' : '60.0%';
      closeConfirmationModal();
      showToast(`Parameter ambang batas PAD ${valT} & ${valM} berhasil diperbarui ke telemetri sistem.`);
    });
  }

  if (btnCloseToast) {
    btnCloseToast.addEventListener('click', hideToast);
  }

  // Reset to default button
  const btnReset = document.getElementById('btn-reset-standar');
  if (btnReset) {
    btnReset.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.value = 80;
      if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.value = 60;
      updateThresholdPreview();
      showToast('Parameter dikembalikan ke standar Perbup OKU Selatan (80.0% & 60.0%).');
    });
  }
</script></body></html>

<script>
  // Drawer interactions
  const openBtn = document.getElementById('btn-open-drawer');
  const closeBtn = document.getElementById('btn-close-drawer');
  const drawer = document.getElementById('nav-drawer');
  const backdrop = document.getElementById('nav-drawer-backdrop');

  function toggleDrawer(open) {
    if (open) {
      drawer.classList.remove('-translate-x-full');
      backdrop.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else {
      drawer.classList.add('-translate-x-full');
      backdrop.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }

  if (openBtn) openBtn.addEventListener('click', () => toggleDrawer(true));
  if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => toggleDrawer(false));
  if (backdrop) backdrop.addEventListener('click', () => toggleDrawer(false));

  // Slider visual simulation and sync
  const sliderTercapai = document.getElementById('slider-tercapai');
  const sliderMendekati = document.getElementById('slider-mendekati');
  const labelTercapai = document.getElementById('label-tercapai');
  const labelMendekati = document.getElementById('label-mendekati');
  const trackKritis = document.getElementById('track-kritis');
  const trackMendekati = document.getElementById('track-mendekati');
  const trackTercapai = document.getElementById('track-tercapai');
  const tagKritis = document.getElementById('tag-kritis');
  const tagMendekati = document.getElementById('tag-mendekati');
  const tagTercapai = document.getElementById('tag-tercapai');
  const kritisDescLabel = document.getElementById('kritis-desc-label');
  const errorAmbangConflict = document.getElementById('error-ambang-conflict');
  const cardAmbang = document.getElementById('card-ambang');

  // Input fields for validation
  const selectTahunAnggaran = document.getElementById('select-tahun-anggaran');
  const errorTahunAnggaran = document.getElementById('error-tahun-anggaran');
  const inputPejabatNama = document.getElementById('input-pejabat-nama');
  const inputPejabatNip = document.getElementById('input-pejabat-nip');
  const errorPejabat = document.getElementById('error-pejabat');
  const cardPejabat = document.getElementById('card-pejabat');
  const avatarInitials = document.getElementById('avatar-initials');

  // Banner elements
  const validationBanner = document.getElementById('validation-banner');
  const validationList = document.getElementById('validation-list');
  const btnCloseValidationBanner = document.getElementById('btn-close-validation-banner');

  function updateInitials() {
    if (!inputPejabatNama || !avatarInitials) return;
    const parts = inputPejabatNama.value.trim().split(' ').filter(p => !p.includes('.') && p.length > 1);
    if (parts.length >= 2) {
      avatarInitials.innerText = (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
    } else if (inputPejabatNama.value.trim().length > 0) {
      avatarInitials.innerText = inputPejabatNama.value.trim().substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
    } else {
      avatarInitials.innerText = '--';
    }
  }

  if (inputPejabatNama) inputPejabatNama.addEventListener('input', updateInitials);

  function updateThresholdPreview() {
    let tVal = parseFloat(sliderTercapai.value);
    let mVal = parseFloat(sliderMendekati.value);

    labelTercapai.innerText = tVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    labelMendekati.innerText = mVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    if (kritisDescLabel) kritisDescLabel.innerText = 'Ambang Kritis: < ' + mVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    
    // Live conflict detection preview
    if (tVal <= mVal) {
      if (errorAmbangConflict) errorAmbangConflict.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.classList.add('accent-error');
      if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.classList.add('accent-error');
    } else {
      if (errorAmbangConflict) errorAmbangConflict.classList.add('hidden');
      if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.classList.remove('accent-error');
      if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.classList.remove('accent-error');
    }

    // update preview track
    if (tVal > mVal) {
      if (trackKritis) trackKritis.style.width = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, mVal)) + '%';
      if (trackMendekati) trackMendekati.style.width = Math.max(0, tVal - mVal) + '%';
      if (trackTercapai) trackTercapai.style.width = Math.max(0, 100 - tVal) + '%';

      if (tagKritis) tagKritis.innerText = '<' + Math.round(mVal) + '%';
      if (tagMendekati) tagMendekati.innerText = Math.round(mVal) + '-' + Math.round(tVal - 1) + '%';
      if (tagTercapai) tagTercapai.innerText = '≥' + Math.round(tVal) + '%';
    } else {
      // Inverted error representation
      if (trackKritis) trackKritis.style.width = '100%';
      if (trackMendekati) trackMendekati.style.width = '0%';
      if (trackTercapai) trackTercapai.style.width = '0%';
      if (tagKritis) tagKritis.innerText = 'Invalid';
      if (tagMendekati) tagMendekati.innerText = '--';
      if (tagTercapai) tagTercapai.innerText = '--';
    }

    // Modal elements sync
    const modalValT = document.getElementById('modal-val-tercapai');
    const modalValM = document.getElementById('modal-val-mendekati');
    if (modalValT) modalValT.innerText = tVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    if (modalValM) modalValM.innerText = mVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
  }

  if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.addEventListener('input', updateThresholdPreview);
  if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.addEventListener('input', updateThresholdPreview);

  // Frequency button switching
  const freqButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.freq-btn');
  freqButtons.forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      freqButtons.forEach(b => {
        b.className = 'freq-btn px-space-sm py-space-xs rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface-variant font-label-status text-label-status text-center hover:bg-surface-container';
        b.innerHTML = b.getAttribute('data-freq');
      });
      btn.className = 'freq-btn px-space-sm py-space-xs rounded-lg bg-primary text-on-primary font-label-status text-label-status text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs';
      btn.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">check_circle</span> ${btn.getAttribute('data-freq')}`;
    });
  });

  // Comprehensive Form Validation Logic
  function validateSettingsForm() {
    const errors = [];
    const tVal = parseFloat(sliderTercapai.value);
    const mVal = parseFloat(sliderMendekati.value);

    // Reset visual error states
    if (errorAmbangConflict) errorAmbangConflict.classList.add('hidden');
    if (errorTahunAnggaran) errorTahunAnggaran.classList.add('hidden');
    if (selectTahunAnggaran) selectTahunAnggaran.classList.remove('border-error');
    if (errorPejabat) errorPejabat.classList.add('hidden');
    if (inputPejabatNama) inputPejabatNama.classList.remove('border-error');
    if (inputPejabatNip) inputPejabatNip.classList.remove('border-error');

    // 1. Ambang Optimum vs Waspada
    if (isNaN(tVal) || isNaN(mVal)) {
      errors.push('Nilai ambang batas harus berupa angka numerik.');
    } else {
      if (tVal <= mVal) {
        errors.push(`Ambang Target Tercapai (${tVal.toFixed(1)}%) harus lebih tinggi dari Ambang Waspada (${mVal.toFixed(1)}%).`);
        if (errorAmbangConflict) errorAmbangConflict.classList.remove('hidden');
      }
      if (tVal < 1 || tVal > 100) {
        errors.push('Ambang Target Tercapai harus berada pada rentang valid 1% s/d 100%.');
      }
      if (mVal < 1 || mVal > 100) {
        errors.push('Ambang Waspada harus berada pada rentang valid 1% s/d 100%.');
      }
    }

    // 2. Tahun Anggaran Aktif
    if (!selectTahunAnggaran || !selectTahunAnggaran.value.trim()) {
      errors.push('Tahun Anggaran Aktif belum dipilih.');
      if (errorTahunAnggaran) errorTahunAnggaran.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (selectTahunAnggaran) selectTahunAnggaran.classList.add('border-error');
    }

    // 3. Pejabat Penandatangan
    const namaPejabat = inputPejabatNama ? inputPejabatNama.value.trim() : '';
    const nipPejabat = inputPejabatNip ? inputPejabatNip.value.trim() : '';
    if (!namaPejabat || !nipPejabat) {
      errors.push('Data Pejabat Penandatangan (Nama Lengkap dan NIP) wajib diisi lengkap.');
      if (errorPejabat) errorPejabat.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (!namaPejabat && inputPejabatNama) inputPejabatNama.classList.add('border-error');
      if (!nipPejabat && inputPejabatNip) inputPejabatNip.classList.add('border-error');
    }

    return {
      isValid: errors.length === 0,
      errors: errors,
      data: {
        tercapai: tVal,
        mendekati: mVal,
        tahunAnggaran: selectTahunAnggaran ? selectTahunAnggaran.value : '',
        pejabat: namaPejabat
      }
    };
  }

  function displayValidationErrors(errors) {
    if (!validationBanner || !validationList) return;
    validationList.innerHTML = '';
    errors.forEach(err => {
      const li = document.createElement('li');
      li.innerText = err;
      validationList.appendChild(li);
    });
    validationBanner.classList.remove('hidden');
    validationBanner.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
  }

  function hideValidationErrors() {
    if (validationBanner) {
      validationBanner.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }

  if (btnCloseValidationBanner) {
    btnCloseValidationBanner.addEventListener('click', hideValidationErrors);
  }

  // Clear errors when fields change
  if (selectTahunAnggaran) {
    selectTahunAnggaran.addEventListener('change', () => {
      if (selectTahunAnggaran.value.trim()) {
        if (errorTahunAnggaran) errorTahunAnggaran.classList.add('hidden');
        selectTahunAnggaran.classList.remove('border-error');
      }
    });
  }
  if (inputPejabatNama) {
    inputPejabatNama.addEventListener('input', () => {
      if (inputPejabatNama.value.trim()) {
        inputPejabatNama.classList.remove('border-error');
        if (inputPejabatNip && inputPejabatNip.value.trim() && errorPejabat) {
          errorPejabat.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      }
    });
  }
  if (inputPejabatNip) {
    inputPejabatNip.addEventListener('input', () => {
      if (inputPejabatNip.value.trim()) {
        inputPejabatNip.classList.remove('border-error');
        if (inputPejabatNama && inputPejabatNama.value.trim() && errorPejabat) {
          errorPejabat.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      }
    });
  }

  // Modal & Toast elements
  const modal = document.getElementById('modal-konfirmasi');
  const modalContent = document.getElementById('modal-konfirmasi-content');
  const btnTrigger = document.getElementById('btn-trigger-save-modal');
  const btnCloseX = document.getElementById('btn-close-modal-x');
  const btnCancel = document.getElementById('btn-cancel-modal');
  const btnConfirm = document.getElementById('btn-confirm-submit');
  const toast = document.getElementById('toast-success-config');
  const btnCloseToast = document.getElementById('btn-close-toast');
  const toastDesc = document.getElementById('toast-desc');

  let toastTimer = null;

  function showToast(message) {
    if (toastDesc && message) {
      toastDesc.innerText = message;
    }
    if (toast) {
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2', 'pointer-events-none');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
      if (toastTimer) clearTimeout(toastTimer);
      toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        hideToast();
      }, 5000);
    }
  }

  function hideToast() {
    if (toast) {
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2', 'pointer-events-none');
    }
  }

  function openConfirmationModal(validatedData) {
    hideValidationErrors();
    
    // Update validated summary values in modal
    const modalValT = document.getElementById('modal-val-tercapai');
    const modalValM = document.getElementById('modal-val-mendekati');
    const modalValTA = document.getElementById('modal-val-ta');
    const modalValPejabat = document.getElementById('modal-val-pejabat');

    if (modalValT) modalValT.innerText = validatedData.tercapai.toFixed(1) + '%';
    if (modalValM) modalValM.innerText = validatedData.mendekati.toFixed(1) + '%';
    if (modalValTA) modalValTA.innerText = validatedData.tahunAnggaran || 'TA 2024';
    if (modalValPejabat) modalValPejabat.innerText = validatedData.pejabat || '-';

    if (modal) {
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        modal.classList.add('opacity-100');
        if (modalContent) {
          modalContent.classList.remove('scale-95');
          modalContent.classList.add('scale-100');
        }
      });
    }
  }

  function closeConfirmationModal() {
    if (modal) {
      modal.classList.remove('opacity-100');
      modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      if (modalContent) {
        modalContent.classList.remove('scale-100');
        modalContent.classList.add('scale-95');
      }
      setTimeout(() => {
        modal.classList.add('hidden');
      }, 300);
    }
  }

  // Handle Save Button with Interactive Validation
  if (btnTrigger) {
    btnTrigger.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const validationResult = validateSettingsForm();
      if (!validationResult.isValid) {
        // Validation failed: do not open modal, show error feedback
        displayValidationErrors(validationResult.errors);
      } else {
        // Validation passed: open confirmation modal with validated values
        openConfirmationModal(validationResult.data);
      }
    });
  }

  if (btnCloseX) btnCloseX.addEventListener('click', closeConfirmationModal);
  if (btnCancel) btnCancel.addEventListener('click', closeConfirmationModal);
  if (modal) {
    modal.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === modal) {
        closeConfirmationModal();
      }
    });
  }

  if (btnConfirm) {
    btnConfirm.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const valT = sliderTercapai ? parseFloat(sliderTercapai.value).toFixed(1) + '%' : '80.0%';
      const valM = sliderMendekati ? parseFloat(sliderMendekati.value).toFixed(1) + '%' : '60.0%';
      closeConfirmationModal();
      showToast(`Parameter ambang batas PAD ${valT} & ${valM} serta konfigurasi TA berhasil divalidasi dan disimpan.`);
    });
  }

  if (btnCloseToast) {
    btnCloseToast.addEventListener('click', hideToast);
  }

  // Reset to default button
  const btnReset = document.getElementById('btn-reset-standar');
  if (btnReset) {
    btnReset.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.value = 80;
      if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.value = 60;
      if (selectTahunAnggaran) selectTahunAnggaran.value = 'TA 2024 (APBD Murni OKU Selatan)';
      if (inputPejabatNama) inputPejabatNama.value = 'Drs. H. Ahmad Marzuki, M.M.';
      if (inputPejabatNip) inputPejabatNip.value = 'NIP. 19740815 199803 1 002';
      updateInitials();
      updateThresholdPreview();
      hideValidationErrors();
      showToast('Parameter dikembalikan ke standar Perbup OKU Selatan (80.0% & 60.0%).');
    });
  }
</script></body></html>

<!-- Pengaturan Sistem PAD OKUS -->

<!-- Banner Peringatan Validasi Interaktif -->
<div class="hidden flex-col gap-space-xs p-space-sm bg-error-container/30 border border-error/30 rounded-xl transition-all duration-300" id="validation-banner" role="alert">
<div class="flex items-start gap-space-xs">

</div></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-40 pb-safe bg-surface/80 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_1px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-semibold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-16 px-space-xs"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="dashboard" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">pie_chart</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Dashboard</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="data-pendapatan" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">table_chart</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Data</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="analisis-pad" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">query_stats</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Analisis</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="laporan" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">description</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Laporan</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs min-w-[56px] h-12 text-primary font-semibold transition-colors" data-path="pengaturan" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">settings</span><span class="font-label-status text-label-status">Pengaturan</span></a></div></nav>

<div class="fixed top-4 inset-x-4 z-50 max-w-sm mx-auto bg-surface-container-lowest border border-tertiary/30 shadow-[0_10px_25px_rgba(0,0,0,0.12)] rounded-xl p-space-sm flex items-start gap-space-sm transition-all duration-300 transform -translate-y-2 opacity-0 pointer-events-none" id="toast-success-config">
<div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-tertiary/10 text-tertiary flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5">

      Parameter ambang batas PAD 80% &amp; 60% berhasil diperbarui ke telemetri sistem.

          </span>

<!-- Notice Box -->

          Perubahan ambang batas ini akan langsung memengaruhi indikator status visual pada Dashboard Utama, Analisis Kinerja, dan Laporan Realisasi PAD se-Kabupaten OKU Selatan.

<!-- Checkbox Persetujuan -->
<label class="flex items-start gap-space-xs cursor-pointer pt-space-xxs">

<span class="font-body-sm text-[11px] text-on-surface leading-snug">Saya menyatakan konfigurasi ini telah sesuai arahan teknis pimpinan Bapenda OKU Selatan.</span>

<!-- Modal Footer / Action Buttons -->
<div class="p-space-md pt-0 flex flex-col gap-space-xs">
<button class="w-full h-10 flex items-center justify-center gap-space-xs bg-primary text-on-primary rounded-lg font-title-sm text-title-sm shadow-sm active:scale-[0.98] transition-all cursor-pointer" id="btn-confirm-submit" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">check_circle</span>
<span class="">Ya, Terapkan Konfigurasi</span>
</button>
<button class="w-full h-9 flex items-center justify-center bg-surface-container text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface rounded-lg font-body-sm text-body-sm active:scale-[0.98] transition-transform cursor-pointer" id="btn-cancel-modal" type="button">
        Batal / Kembali
      </button>
</div>

<script>
  // Drawer interactions
  const openBtn = document.getElementById('btn-open-drawer');
  const closeBtn = document.getElementById('btn-close-drawer');
  const drawer = document.getElementById('nav-drawer');
  const backdrop = document.getElementById('nav-drawer-backdrop');

  function toggleDrawer(open) {
    if (open) {
      drawer.classList.remove('-translate-x-full');
      backdrop.classList.remove('hidden');
    } else {
      drawer.classList.add('-translate-x-full');
      backdrop.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }

  if (openBtn) openBtn.addEventListener('click', () => toggleDrawer(true));
  if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => toggleDrawer(false));
  if (backdrop) backdrop.addEventListener('click', () => toggleDrawer(false));

  // Slider & manual input visual simulation and sync
  const sliderTercapai = document.getElementById('slider-tercapai');
  const sliderMendekati = document.getElementById('slider-mendekati');
  const inputTercapai = document.getElementById('input-tercapai');
  const inputMendekati = document.getElementById('input-mendekati');
  const trackKritis = document.getElementById('track-kritis');
  const trackMendekati = document.getElementById('track-mendekati');
  const trackTercapai = document.getElementById('track-tercapai');
  const tagKritis = document.getElementById('tag-kritis');
  const tagMendekati = document.getElementById('tag-mendekati');
  const tagTercapai = document.getElementById('tag-tercapai');
  const kritisDescLabel = document.getElementById('kritis-desc-label');
  const errorAmbangConflict = document.getElementById('error-ambang-conflict');
  const cardAmbang = document.getElementById('card-ambang');

  // Input fields for validation
  const selectTahunAnggaran = document.getElementById('select-tahun-anggaran');
  const errorTahunAnggaran = document.getElementById('error-tahun-anggaran');
  const inputPejabatNama = document.getElementById('input-pejabat-nama');
  const inputPejabatNip = document.getElementById('input-pejabat-nip');
  const errorPejabat = document.getElementById('error-pejabat');
  const cardPejabat = document.getElementById('card-pejabat');
  const avatarInitials = document.getElementById('avatar-initials');

  // Banner elements
  const validationBanner = document.getElementById('validation-banner');
  const validationList = document.getElementById('validation-list');
  const btnCloseValidationBanner = document.getElementById('btn-close-validation-banner');

  function updateInitials() {
    if (!inputPejabatNama || !avatarInitials) return;
    const parts = inputPejabatNama.value.trim().split(' ').filter(p => !p.includes('.') && p.length > 1);
    if (parts.length >= 2) {
      avatarInitials.innerText = (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();
    } else if (inputPejabatNama.value.trim().length > 0) {
      avatarInitials.innerText = inputPejabatNama.value.trim().substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
    } else {
      avatarInitials.innerText = '--';
    }
  }

  if (inputPejabatNama) inputPejabatNama.addEventListener('input', updateInitials);

  function updateThresholdPreview(source) {
    let tVal = parseFloat(sliderTercapai.value);
    let mVal = parseFloat(sliderMendekati.value);

    if (source === 'input-tercapai' && inputTercapai) {
      tVal = parseFloat(inputTercapai.value);
      if (!isNaN(tVal)) {
        sliderTercapai.value = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, tVal));
      }
    } else if (inputTercapai && !isNaN(tVal)) {
      if (document.activeElement !== inputTercapai) {
        inputTercapai.value = tVal.toFixed(1);
      }
    }

    if (source === 'input-mendekati' && inputMendekati) {
      mVal = parseFloat(inputMendekati.value);
      if (!isNaN(mVal)) {
        sliderMendekati.value = Math.min(100, Math.max(1, mVal));
      }
    } else if (inputMendekati && !isNaN(mVal)) {
      if (document.activeElement !== inputMendekati) {
        inputMendekati.value = mVal.toFixed(1);
      }
    }

    tVal = parseFloat(sliderTercapai.value) || 0;
    mVal = parseFloat(sliderMendekati.value) || 0;

    if (kritisDescLabel) kritisDescLabel.innerText = 'Ambang Kritis: < ' + mVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    
    // Live conflict detection preview
    if (tVal <= mVal) {
      if (errorAmbangConflict) errorAmbangConflict.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.classList.add('accent-error');
      if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.classList.add('accent-error');
    } else {
      if (errorAmbangConflict) errorAmbangConflict.classList.add('hidden');
      if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.classList.remove('accent-error');
      if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.classList.remove('accent-error');
    }

    // update preview track
    if (tVal > mVal) {
      if (trackKritis) trackKritis.style.width = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, mVal)) + '%';
      if (trackMendekati) trackMendekati.style.width = Math.max(0, tVal - mVal) + '%';
      if (trackTercapai) trackTercapai.style.width = Math.max(0, 100 - tVal) + '%';

      if (tagKritis) tagKritis.innerText = '<' + Math.round(mVal) + '%';
      if (tagMendekati) tagMendekati.innerText = Math.round(mVal) + '-' + Math.round(tVal - 1) + '%';
      if (tagTercapai) tagTercapai.innerText = '≥' + Math.round(tVal) + '%';
    } else {
      // Inverted error representation
      if (trackKritis) trackKritis.style.width = '100%';
      if (trackMendekati) trackMendekati.style.width = '0%';
      if (trackTercapai) trackTercapai.style.width = '0%';
      if (tagKritis) tagKritis.innerText = 'Invalid';
      if (tagMendekati) tagMendekati.innerText = '--';
      if (tagTercapai) tagTercapai.innerText = '--';
    }

    // Modal elements sync
    const modalValT = document.getElementById('modal-val-tercapai');
    const modalValM = document.getElementById('modal-val-mendekati');
    if (modalValT) modalValT.innerText = tVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
    if (modalValM) modalValM.innerText = mVal.toFixed(1) + '%';
  }

  if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.addEventListener('input', () => updateThresholdPreview('slider-tercapai'));
  if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.addEventListener('input', () => updateThresholdPreview('slider-mendekati'));
  
  if (inputTercapai) {
    inputTercapai.addEventListener('input', () => updateThresholdPreview('input-tercapai'));
    inputTercapai.addEventListener('blur', () => {
      let val = parseFloat(inputTercapai.value);
      if (isNaN(val) || val < 1) val = 1;
      if (val > 100) val = 100;
      inputTercapai.value = val.toFixed(1);
      sliderTercapai.value = val;
      updateThresholdPreview();
    });
  }

  if (inputMendekati) {
    inputMendekati.addEventListener('input', () => updateThresholdPreview('input-mendekati'));
    inputMendekati.addEventListener('blur', () => {
      let val = parseFloat(inputMendekati.value);
      if (isNaN(val) || val < 1) val = 1;
      if (val > 100) val = 100;
      inputMendekati.value = val.toFixed(1);
      sliderMendekati.value = val;
      updateThresholdPreview();
    });
  }

  // Frequency button switching
  const freqButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.freq-btn');
  freqButtons.forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      freqButtons.forEach(b => {
        b.className = 'freq-btn px-space-sm py-space-xs rounded-lg bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface-variant font-label-status text-label-status text-center hover:bg-surface-container';
        b.innerHTML = b.getAttribute('data-freq');
      });
      btn.className = 'freq-btn px-space-sm py-space-xs rounded-lg bg-primary text-on-primary font-label-status text-label-status text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-space-xxs';
      btn.innerHTML = `<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">check_circle</span> ${btn.getAttribute('data-freq')}`;
    });
  });

  // Comprehensive Form Validation Logic
  function validateSettingsForm() {
    const errors = [];
    const tVal = parseFloat(inputTercapai ? inputTercapai.value : sliderTercapai.value);
    const mVal = parseFloat(inputMendekati ? inputMendekati.value : sliderMendekati.value);

    // Reset visual error states
    if (errorAmbangConflict) errorAmbangConflict.classList.add('hidden');
    if (errorTahunAnggaran) errorTahunAnggaran.classList.add('hidden');
    if (selectTahunAnggaran) selectTahunAnggaran.classList.remove('border-error');
    if (errorPejabat) errorPejabat.classList.add('hidden');
    if (inputPejabatNama) inputPejabatNama.classList.remove('border-error');
    if (inputPejabatNip) inputPejabatNip.classList.remove('border-error');

    // 1. Ambang Optimum vs Waspada
    if (isNaN(tVal) || isNaN(mVal)) {
      errors.push('Nilai ambang batas harus berupa angka numerik.');
    } else {
      if (tVal <= mVal) {
        errors.push(`Ambang Target Tercapai (${tVal.toFixed(1)}%) harus lebih tinggi dari Ambang Waspada (${mVal.toFixed(1)}%).`);
        if (errorAmbangConflict) errorAmbangConflict.classList.remove('hidden');
      }
      if (tVal < 1 || tVal > 100) {
        errors.push('Ambang Target Tercapai harus berada pada rentang valid 1% s/d 100%.');
      }
      if (mVal < 1 || mVal > 100) {
        errors.push('Ambang Waspada harus berada pada rentang valid 1% s/d 100%.');
      }
    }

    // 2. Tahun Anggaran Aktif
    if (!selectTahunAnggaran || !selectTahunAnggaran.value.trim()) {
      errors.push('Tahun Anggaran Aktif belum dipilih.');
      if (errorTahunAnggaran) errorTahunAnggaran.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (selectTahunAnggaran) selectTahunAnggaran.classList.add('border-error');
    }

    // 3. Pejabat Penandatangan
    const namaPejabat = inputPejabatNama ? inputPejabatNama.value.trim() : '';
    const nipPejabat = inputPejabatNip ? inputPejabatNip.value.trim() : '';
    if (!namaPejabat || !nipPejabat) {
      errors.push('Data Pejabat Penandatangan (Nama Lengkap dan NIP) wajib diisi lengkap.');
      if (errorPejabat) errorPejabat.classList.remove('hidden');
      if (!namaPejabat && inputPejabatNama) inputPejabatNama.classList.add('border-error');
      if (!nipPejabat && inputPejabatNip) inputPejabatNip.classList.add('border-error');
    }

    return {
      isValid: errors.length === 0,
      errors: errors,
      data: {
        tercapai: tVal,
        mendekati: mVal,
        tahunAnggaran: selectTahunAnggaran ? selectTahunAnggaran.value : '',
        pejabat: namaPejabat
      }
    };
  }

  function displayValidationErrors(errors) {
    if (!validationBanner || !validationList) return;
    validationList.innerHTML = '';
    errors.forEach(err => {
      const li = document.createElement('li');
      li.innerText = err;
      validationList.appendChild(li);
    });
    validationBanner.classList.remove('hidden');
    validationBanner.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
  }

  function hideValidationErrors() {
    if (validationBanner) {
      validationBanner.classList.add('hidden');
    }
  }

  if (btnCloseValidationBanner) {
    btnCloseValidationBanner.addEventListener('click', hideValidationErrors);
  }

  // Clear errors when fields change
  if (selectTahunAnggaran) {
    selectTahunAnggaran.addEventListener('change', () => {
      if (selectTahunAnggaran.value.trim()) {
        if (errorTahunAnggaran) errorTahunAnggaran.classList.add('hidden');
        selectTahunAnggaran.classList.remove('border-error');
      }
    });
  }
  if (inputPejabatNama) {
    inputPejabatNama.addEventListener('input', () => {
      if (inputPejabatNama.value.trim()) {
        inputPejabatNama.classList.remove('border-error');
        if (inputPejabatNip && inputPejabatNip.value.trim() && errorPejabat) {
          errorPejabat.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      }
    });
  }
  if (inputPejabatNip) {
    inputPejabatNip.addEventListener('input', () => {
      if (inputPejabatNip.value.trim()) {
        inputPejabatNip.classList.remove('border-error');
        if (inputPejabatNama && inputPejabatNama.value.trim() && errorPejabat) {
          errorPejabat.classList.add('hidden');
        }
      }
    });
  }

  // Modal & Toast elements
  const modal = document.getElementById('modal-konfirmasi');
  const modalContent = document.getElementById('modal-konfirmasi-content');
  const btnTrigger = document.getElementById('btn-trigger-save-modal');
  const btnCloseX = document.getElementById('btn-close-modal-x');
  const btnCancel = document.getElementById('btn-cancel-modal');
  const btnConfirm = document.getElementById('btn-confirm-submit');
  const toast = document.getElementById('toast-success-config');
  const btnCloseToast = document.getElementById('btn-close-toast');
  const toastDesc = document.getElementById('toast-desc');

  let toastTimer = null;

  function showToast(message) {
    if (toastDesc && message) {
      toastDesc.innerText = message;
    }
    if (toast) {
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2', 'pointer-events-none');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
      if (toastTimer) clearTimeout(toastTimer);
      toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        hideToast();
      }, 5000);
    }
  }

  function hideToast() {
    if (toast) {
      toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
      toast.classList.add('opacity-0', '-translate-y-2', 'pointer-events-none');
    }
  }

  function openConfirmationModal(validatedData) {
    hideValidationErrors();
    
    // Update validated summary values in modal
    const modalValT = document.getElementById('modal-val-tercapai');
    const modalValM = document.getElementById('modal-val-mendekati');
    const modalValTA = document.getElementById('modal-val-ta');
    const modalValPejabat = document.getElementById('modal-val-pejabat');

    if (modalValT) modalValT.innerText = validatedData.tercapai.toFixed(1) + '%';
    if (modalValM) modalValM.innerText = validatedData.mendekati.toFixed(1) + '%';
    if (modalValTA) modalValTA.innerText = validatedData.tahunAnggaran || 'TA 2024';
    if (modalValPejabat) modalValPejabat.innerText = validatedData.pejabat || '-';

    if (modal) {
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        modal.classList.remove('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        modal.classList.add('opacity-100');
        if (modalContent) {
          modalContent.classList.remove('scale-95');
          modalContent.classList.add('scale-100');
        }
      });
    }
  }

  function closeConfirmationModal() {
    if (modal) {
      modal.classList.remove('opacity-100');
      modal.classList.add('opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
      if (modalContent) {
        modalContent.classList.remove('scale-100');
        modalContent.classList.add('scale-95');
      }
      setTimeout(() => {
        modal.classList.add('hidden');
      }, 300);
    }
  }

  // Handle Save Button with Interactive Validation
  if (btnTrigger) {
    btnTrigger.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const validationResult = validateSettingsForm();
      if (!validationResult.isValid) {
        // Validation failed: do not open modal, show error feedback
        displayValidationErrors(validationResult.errors);
      } else {
        // Validation passed: open confirmation modal with validated values
        openConfirmationModal(validationResult.data);
      }
    });
  }

  if (btnCloseX) btnCloseX.addEventListener('click', closeConfirmationModal);
  if (btnCancel) btnCancel.addEventListener('click', closeConfirmationModal);
  if (modal) {
    modal.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (e.target === modal) {
        closeConfirmationModal();
      }
    });
  }

  if (btnConfirm) {
    btnConfirm.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const valT = inputTercapai ? parseFloat(inputTercapai.value).toFixed(1) + '%' : (sliderTercapai ? parseFloat(sliderTercapai.value).toFixed(1) + '%' : '80.0%');
      const valM = inputMendekati ? parseFloat(inputMendekati.value).toFixed(1) + '%' : (sliderMendekati ? parseFloat(sliderMendekati.value).toFixed(1) + '%' : '60.0%');
      closeConfirmationModal();
      showToast(`Parameter ambang batas PAD ${valT} & ${valM} serta konfigurasi TA berhasil divalidasi dan disimpan.`);
    });
  }

  if (btnCloseToast) {
    btnCloseToast.addEventListener('click', hideToast);
  }

  // Reset to default button
  const btnReset = document.getElementById('btn-reset-standar');
  if (btnReset) {
    btnReset.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (sliderTercapai) sliderTercapai.value = 80;
      if (sliderMendekati) sliderMendekati.value = 60;
      if (inputTercapai) inputTercapai.value = '80.0';
      if (inputMendekati) inputMendekati.value = '60.0';
      if (selectTahunAnggaran) selectTahunAnggaran.value = 'TA 2024 (APBD Murni OKU Selatan)';
      if (inputPejabatNama) inputPejabatNama.value = 'Drs. H. Ahmad Marzuki, M.M.';
      if (inputPejabatNip) inputPejabatNip.value = 'NIP. 19740815 199803 1 002';
      updateInitials();
      updateThresholdPreview();
      hideValidationErrors();
      showToast('Parameter dikembalikan ke standar Perbup OKU Selatan (80.0% & 60.0%).');
    });
  }
</script></body></html>
